# Requirement coverage

One row per clause in the specification, sub-clauses included. `MET at <path>` means the cited
module implements the clause and production code reaches what is cited; `PARTIAL` says which half is
there; `MISSING` is work; `OUT OF SCOPE` states a reason and keeps the clause (Appendix C). Re-mark
in the same change that meets a requirement, and recount with `npm run check:existence` rather than
adjusting a tally.

A sub-clause is answered on its own. It takes the mark of the clause above it only when its own
sentence is true of the source; otherwise it says what of itself is short, in its own words.

`MET` is **a claim about the SOURCE, not about the world.** `npm run check:existence` opens every
cited path and refuses a MET naming an item no production code calls, so the claim can be wrong;
`npm run coverage:spec` names any clause with no row at all.

A row says what the source does and what of the clause is short. It never says who will fix it: the
work lives in `docs/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, that file changes every week, and a pointer from here into
it is dead the week after it is written. It carries no history either — what a change found belongs
in the project record, and what is TRUE belongs here (Appendix C).

## Money

| requirement  | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------ | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Money A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs (`issue` asserts an issuer at the site; `owed_by` reads what an issuer owes from what others hold of what it issued)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Money A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs keys a money holding by its holder and by the deposit line itself, and packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issuer_of` names the bank that issued that line. A deposit is one named bank owing one named holder, and neither end can be blank                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Money A1.b` | MET     | a central bank issues its own money line through packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issue`, and its holders are the parties whose profile banks nowhere else. A reserve balance is that issuer owing a named bank, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/bank_funding.rs reads exactly that relation when it draws on the facility                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Money A1.c` | MISSING | two money lines exist, a central-bank reserve line and each bank's deposit line, and neither is currency in circulation. No third money line is issued to a holder outside the banks, so the clause has no instrument to be true of                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Money A1.d` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issue` refuses an instrument with no issuer, and packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs refuses a `Leg::Mint` whose `issuer` is not the money`s own, whose instrument is not money, or whose amount is not positive — the one leg in this engine that CREATES money. `Leg::Create` is refused on a money line in the same pass, which is the other side of that division                                                                                                                         |
| `Money A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs carries a `ccy` on every instrument and `ccy_of` is the one read of it, so a money line names the currency it is denominated in and no caller has to infer one                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Money A2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `account_of` answers which money a party holds, its own bank's or its own issue where it banks nowhere, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/money.rs `statements` keys every book by owner and currency together. A party reports in the money it is paid in                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Money A2.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/num.rs `MoneyAmount` carries its `CurrencyCode`, exposes no unchecked addition and refuses `checked_add` across currencies, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/money.rs `Owed` uses it in production to aggregate issuer liabilities per currency rather than across currencies. The one door that restates an amount in another money takes an explicit rate, and no production code opens it                                                                                                          |
| `Money A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `account_of` (which money a party holds — its bank`s, or its own where it banks nowhere), packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs (a row is keyed by instrument, and an instrument is one issuer`s one money)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

| `Money B3`   | PARTIAL | one holder's answer exists: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/bank_funding.rs turns a bank reserve shortfall into a collateralised central-bank facility with a priced claim and minted reserves. | `Money F4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs records `NoAccountInThatMoney` rather than applying or dropping a payment whose destination account does not exist, and packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs reconciles that wire outcome with the scheduled obligation.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Money G1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/calendar.rs `Calendar` carries an epoch and a `Week` and nothing finer, so there is no structure inside a period for anything to happen in. packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `World::step` settles once, and packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `Settlement::waits_for` counts whole weeks, so a payment that cannot be made waits a whole one                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Money G1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/calendar.rs is the one mapping and `year_fraction` computes from dates, and nothing orders one event before another by date: packages/kernel-rs/src/world.rs `Phases` orders by stage alone                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Money G1.b` | MET     | **the stage order IS the record date.** packages/kernel-rs/src/systems.rs puts `lending` in the OWED stage and packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs clears the books two stages later, so `Servicing` reads the register before anything in the week has traded and pays whoever held the line at the week's open. Nothing marks a record date, because there is no moment between the open and the payment in which paper can change hands                                                                                         |
| `Money G1.c` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `MechanismContext::propose` refuses at the site, with its citation, for a mechanism running at `SCHEDULED` — so the stage is a rule rather than a placement. What such a stage produces instead is an obligation through `owes`, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/private_equity.rs `Calling` writes its capital call to fall due at the next period's open                                                                                                                                 |
| `Money G2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/world.rs declares the nine stages as `STAGES` and `Phases::add` refuses a phase outside them; packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `World::step` is ONE pass over them with no predicate and no two halves. Every row that runs a mechanism is placed in a stage; a row whose only act is to post declares none, because when a party may post is the kernel's and not a system's                                                                                                                            |
| `Money G2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `World::opens`: `resting.expire(today)`, then `wire.give_up(…)`, then what was set in flight to close now closes — before any phase reads a book, and a given-up payment is a recorded `Outcome`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Money G2.b` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/systems.rs puts `lending`, `cds`, `loss`, `mortality` and `estate` in `AT_OWED` in that order, so a loss lands and a party ceases before it can trade and the estate sees the deaths it is for. What is missing is the first word of the stage: nothing accrues                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Money G2.c` | MISSING | the stage exists and no row is in it: four of the five cell events have no cause, so the population changes only by SPLIT                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Money G2.d` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/systems.rs `AT_WORK` holds the sixteen rows that make, wear, move, engage and bring paper — `recipe`, `goods`, `employment`, `capital_programme`, `commodities`, `housing`, `freight`, `insurers`, `trade_credit`, `small_business`, `securities_lending`, `securitisation`, `equity`, `treasury`, `short_term_debt`, `corporate_credit` — all of them before the books                                                                                                                                    |
| `Money G2.e` | MET     | the stage is two things and the kernel runs them in order: `expectations` constructs `Forming` once at `AT_VIEWS`, and packages/kernel-rs/src/world.rs `POSTS` — added after every module phase in that stage — is where packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `ask_book` asks every party what it wants. Nothing clears there, so a party reads everything above it and nothing below. Six participants build their level from their own `price_outlook`: households.rs, funds.rs, insurers.rs, dealing.rs, goods.rs and treasury.rs |
| `Money G2.f` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `run_books`, called at the `BOOKS` stage marker — one call, one solver per (market, instrument)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Money G2.g` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/systems.rs `AT_JUDGED` holds the twenty-one rows that mark, derive, test, publish and opine — `firms`, `reporting`, `ratings`, `second_opinion`, `cost_of_capital`, `benchmarks`, `bank_capital`, `bank_funding`, `sovereign`, `derivative_layer`, `spot_fx`, `fx_forwards`, `observer` among them — and every one of them now runs AFTER the books rather than before                                                                                                                                     |
| `Money G2.h` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/systems.rs `AT_SCHEDULED` holds `private_equity`, `forced_sale`, `control` and `polity` — the four that are called in one period and acted on in the next — and packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs refuses an instruction proposed from that stage, so nothing in it can act in the period that produced it                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Money G2.i` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `World::closes`: `wire.unwind(…)` once with every payment of the period in it, then `audit.run(…)` over what the period left                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Money G3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/calendar.rs is the one calendar: one epoch, one `Week`, and every schedule, print and journal row stores that tick and no other. The placement of every periodicity on it BY DATE is absent (G3.a), and the boundary value such a placement would advance — `CivilDate`, and the mapping onto a tick beside it — is named by no production code                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Money G3.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `PaymentFrequency` answers `weeks` with a fixed count — four for a month, thirteen for a quarter, fifty-two for a year — and every recurring payment is placed by adding it. packages/kernel-rs/src/calendar.rs `week_on_or_after` maps a civil date onto a tick and no schedule calls it, so nothing is placed by advancing a date                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Money G3.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `PaymentFrequency` places every recurring payment directly in fixed weekly ticks; no sub-week interval is representable                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Money G3.c` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/calendar.rs `Convention::year_fraction` is the one writer of a year fraction and derives it from `elapsed_days_until`, so every accrual in the tree is days over a year rather than a count of periods                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Money G4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/calendar.rs, packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs and packages/kernel-rs/src/journal.rs use the same canonical `Week` for instructions, settlement and records                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Money G4.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/calendar.rs `Week` derives no `Default`, so no tick means both now and unset                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

## Register

| requirement     | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --------------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Register A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `row` keys a holding by holder and instrument, and `quantity` is that holding in the unit the instrument is divided into. A row is those three things and carries nothing else                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Register A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `open` asserts the holder at the site, and packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `account_of` answers which money that holder is paid in. A row cannot name a party the world does not have                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Register A1.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issue` refuses an instrument with no issuer and `issued_of` carries what that issuer put out, and packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `HoldersAgainstIssued` compares the two every period                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Register A1.c` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs declares a `UnitId` on every instrument, so the unit is part of what the paper IS. It does not travel: `unit_of` is named by no production code, so a quantity is read without asking what one of it is                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Register A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issuer_of` (a holding is a claim on a named issuer, and A2.a`s "pays to whoever the register says holds it, then" is settlement reading the register at the moment it applies). Re-marked from MISSING                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Register A2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Servicing` reads `of_instrument` on the payment date itself, so a coupon or a redemption reaches whoever the register says holds the line at that moment and nobody who held it earlier                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Register A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `open` (asserts the holder at the site)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Register A4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `open`, packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issue` (both assert)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Register B1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issued_of` is written only by settlement through `moves`; packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `Leg::Create`/`Mint` name issuance and reopening, while `Leg::Destroy` with `Gone::Redeemed` names buyback, amortisation and maturity. The production maturity path in `mechanisms/lending.rs::Servicing` and primary-share path in `session.rs` use those same settlement legs.                                                                                                                        |
| `Register B2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `HoldersAgainstIssued` sums the holdings of each line on the audit`s shared walk and compares them with packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issued_of` — two independent numbers, where before the only other one was the sum of the holdings itself (Audit A1.a`s tautology). B2.a decides the message and B2.b the tolerance, which is the dust of its own terms. It reports a standing violation on every line the arbitrary world was seeded with, which is that world measured rather than argued |
| `Register B2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `HoldersAgainstIssued` reports the signed difference between what is held and what was issued, with the line and its size, so a shortfall and a surplus are two different findings and neither is absorbed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Register B2.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/num.rs `dust` is the one tolerance in the tree, terms times epsilon times the magnitudes that were added, and packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs derives it per comparison. No fraction of the issue appears anywhere                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Register B3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `owed_by` derives each issuer liability from holdings of its issued lines rather than storing a second copy; `booked_equity` consumes that read for live balance-sheet liabilities.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Register B4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Servicing` resolves every holding at maturity into principal money and a `Gone::Redeemed` destruction; `mechanisms/loss.rs::Losses` resolves written-off holdings after allocating recovery and loss, while `assembly.rs::convert_dues_to_claims` files unpaid obligations against the named estate or resolution identity.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Register C1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `Leg::Asset` (the seller loses exactly what the buyer gains, both legs in one atomic instruction)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Register C2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `Cause` (trade, payment, corporate action, production, settlement) and `Leg::Asset.price_per_unit` for C2.a                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Register C2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `Leg::Asset` carries `price_per_unit` as an option, so a transfer that is a trade records the level it crossed at and one that is not says so by holding none                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Register C3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `Settlement::attempt` (every leg is pre-checked before any applies, so neither leg happens without the other), `Delivery` (and a delivery with no payment against it must be DECLARED free, which is what makes a dropped payment distinguishable from a legitimate one), `Outcome` (a fail is a named recorded state)                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Register C3.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `Settlement::attempt` pre-checks every leg of an instruction before it applies any of them, so the asset and the cash either both move or neither does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Register C3.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs answers a refused instruction with an `Outcome` naming why — short of units, short of money, no account in that money — and the wire keeps it. Nothing applies half an instruction and says nothing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Register C4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs (`ShortOfUnits`: a holder cannot deliver what it does not hold)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Register C5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `FlowsAreComplete` reads every settled wire leg in the period and accounts equal positive and negative quantities for each `Leg::Asset`; it then compares those flows with the independently walked register across consecutive period boundaries.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

| `Register D2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs keeps `of_holder` and `of_instrument` as two indexes it writes together, so neither direction is reconstructed from the other and neither can drift                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Register D3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `worth` computes quantity times the latest market print at read time (or contractual one only for money), returns `None` when a non-money line has no print, and never falls back to the lot basis; `market_book_value` applies that read across a party`s portfolio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `Register D5.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `free` is the held quantity less what liens encumber, and `debit` draws against that figure, so a pledged unit cannot be delivered to anybody else                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `Register F3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `FlowsAreComplete` retains the independently walked holding state from the preceding consecutive period, derives the current change, and reports any difference not explained by settled wire legs using arithmetic-dust tolerance.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

## Clearing

| requirement     | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| --------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `Clearing A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/protocols.rs `Protocol` gives each venue its own microstructure and `Venue` declares which one a book uses: `Call` is a sealed cross at one level, `Posted` is a seller standing behind an ask against a buyer taking the best it saw, and `Book` is resting orders matched as they arrive at the level the resting side stood at. All three clear real supply against real demand, and packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs dispatches on the declaration rather than on what is traded |
| `Clearing A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs gives every deciding party its own `Outlooks` row formed from its own history, so two parties reading the same book bring different levels. Identical participants would post identical orders and `clear` would find nothing crossing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Clearing A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `Participant` answers with a list of `Order` values rather than one figure, so what a party posts is quantity at level across levels, and packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` is the one solver every venue submits that schedule to                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Clearing A2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `Order` carries a level with its quantity and a participant answers with a list of them, so the book can be asked what happens at a different price. A quantity with no level is admitted only as auction supply, where the buyers name the levels instead                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Clearing A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `ParticipantView` (a schedule is written from that party`s own state and it can see nothing else), packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Clearing A4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` sweeps only levels posted before it runs, and admits an order naming none solely as auction supply under `BuyersCompete`, where the buyers are the ones competing. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/forced_sale.rs posts against the prior public mark its seller can already see, never the result the book is about to produce                                                                                                                                 |
| `Clearing B1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `Order` carries the party that posted it, and packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `run_book` turns each fill into an instruction against the account of that named party, so a balance sheet moves for every trade                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Clearing B2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `ParticipantView`, packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs (a party is asked and answers out of its own reason)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Clearing B3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/dealing.rs `Dealers` posts funded bid and inventory-limited offer schedules from its own capital, holdings and outlook; packages/kernel-rs/src/systems.rs registers it in the running world.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Clearing B3.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/dealing.rs `Lines` holds a per-dealer position limit and `Dealers` reads it before quoting, so a desk is not an unlimited counterparty. The limit is a declared number rather than a read of the capital and the inventory behind it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Clearing B4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` refuses a buy order that names no level, at the site rather than afterwards, so no schedule can read whatever is left at whatever price. An order with no level is admitted only as auction supply, which is the opposite side                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Clearing B5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` sweeps the levels that were posted and adds no order of its own. A side nobody stood on comes back as `NoDemand` or `NoSupply` rather than as demand the mechanism supplied to itself                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Clearing C1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` (one sweep up the posted levels, two pointers, integer quantities)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Clearing C2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` takes its candidate levels from the posted orders themselves and returns `NoOverlap` when none of them crosses, so the price is a root of the schedules and is never assigned                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Clearing C3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `ration` (pro rata by LARGEST REMAINDER in integer pieces, summing to exactly the volume, so no piece is rounded away)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Clearing C4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `Outcome` (Cleared, NoDemand, NoSupply, NoOverlap — all four are representable outcomes)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Clearing C4.a` | PARTIAL | the outcome propagates for one consequence: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs reads a short fill and `handle` takes the issuer to its buffer, a deferred outlay or back to the market. A seller keeping inventory and a maturity left unrolled change nothing for anybody, because no participant reads a failed book                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Clearing C4.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `Outcome` carries `NoDemand`, `NoSupply` and `NoOverlap` beside a cleared price, so a book that did not cross says which of the three it was in its own return value. `NoOverlap` reports the best level each side named and nothing the sweep invented, and no path substitutes a price                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Clearing C4.c` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `bracket` (a failed cross returns `NoOverlap { best_bid, best_ask }`, which is not a Print and cannot be written as one)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Clearing C5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs (`clear` is a pure function of the posted orders and the venue`s rule)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Clearing D1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `Print` (instrument, market, period, price, currency, quoted-as and provenance)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Clearing D1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs stores a price and nothing else, and packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `yield_to` takes a price and returns a yield. Every derived level in the tree runs that way round and no store holds one                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Clearing D2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `Fill`, packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `pair_up`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Clearing D3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `run_book` makes each exchange of asset for cash one atomic settlement instruction, and writes the cleared print only after at least one of them has settled                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Clearing D4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs values market-carried holdings from `Prints::latest`; packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs reports any market-carried position without an observable mark.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Clearing D5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs records bought, sold, cash-paid and cash-received totals from accepted settlement outcomes; `ClearingReconciles` independently checks both equalities per session.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Clearing E1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `Prints` holds every print in one store and `latest` answers any participant or any other market that asks for one                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Clearing E2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `latest` is how a mechanism reads a book it did not run, and derivative_layer.rs, control.rs, cost_of_capital.rs, housing.rs and spot_fx.rs all price off prints produced elsewhere                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Clearing E3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/dealing.rs `Dealers` derives its bid and its offer from its own posted schedule and posts those same levels into the book. No width is stored and none is applied to a mid                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Clearing E4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs writes `Cleared` only after settlement; a book with no settled trade carries the prior print with `Provenance::Carried`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Clearing F1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/world.rs `Phases` holds the order as data and every row declares what it says into the week and what it needs of that week; `seal` refuses a phase placed before the one that produces what it needs, and packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `World::step` runs the sealed order                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Clearing F1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/world.rs `seal` refuses at assembly a phase that needs this week's rows of a kind a later phase writes, and packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs throws where a phase reads a print its week has not produced. The fix available to a writer is the declared order, because there is no forward reference to reach for                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Clearing F2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `write` refuses a second print for one (market, instrument) in one week, and packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `run_book` captures the cleared level once and uses that one level for every settlement and for the resulting mark. One book, one rate for the week it clears in                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Clearing F3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/world.rs `Phases` orders the week as data and `seal` fixes that order against what each phase declared, so moving a producer after its reader changes the available input rather than passing silently — it is refused where the reader needs this week's, and costs it a week where it does not                                                                                                                                                                                      |

## Audit

| requirement  | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `Audit A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Contribution` gives each independent family a read-only source and one period-close pass; the former self-comparison was removed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Audit A1.a` | MET     | every family compares two records that are kept apart: holdings against issued, wire outcomes against schedule states, the residual against the equity account the events moved, a cell`s total against its weight. No contribution reads one thing against itself — `LotsAgainstQuantity`, which did, is gone                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Audit A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Violation` (family, spec, owner, size, unit, period, message — a finding with no owner cannot be chased)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Audit A2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Violation` carries an owner, a size and a period as fields with no absent case, so a finding without a party it belongs to cannot be written down                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Audit A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Violation.size` and `Violation.unit`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Audit A4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs and packages/kernel-rs/src/num.rs `dust` (each family derives its own tolerance from its own terms and magnitudes — terms × ε × Σ of the absolute magnitudes — and tools/phoenix-check refuses a tolerance nobody derived)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Audit A4.a` | MET     | every family derives its tolerance from packages/kernel-rs/src/num.rs `dust`, at the site of the comparison and over the magnitudes it compared. No percentage appears anywhere in packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs, so nothing scales with the defect it is meant to catch                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Audit B1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `MoneyIsConserved` walks every money account per currency against what it held last period, and reads what the issuers made off the wire's own `Leg::Mint` legs. Two independent things: a transfer's legs sum to zero over the currency, so anything left over is money from somewhere other than an issuer (Money A1.d). C4.c's issuance exception is the thing measured rather than a hole, because the mint legs are exactly what the other side counts                                                                                                                  |
| `Audit B2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `HoldersAgainstIssued` sums the holders of each line on the shared walk and compares them with packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issued_of`, with the dust of its own terms. `LotsAgainstQuantity` is gone: it compared a row with itself and could not fail                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Audit B3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `MarketValuesExist` reports every market-carried holding for which the applicable cleared or carried print is absent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Audit B4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `CrossMarketValues` independently reconciles each cleared session with the price-store route to the same instrument, market and period.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Audit B5`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Equity` keeps each party's account as the movements that made it and packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `AccountsBalance` reports the difference against the residual, with the dust of the two walks that produced them. What is short is on the account's side: settlement moves it for capital paid in, for income and cost receipts and for what a disposal realised, and assembly for what an exhausted estate did not pay, so units destroyed with no proceeds, transfers with no consideration and a mark that moves are changes to the residual that reach no account |
| `Audit B5.a` | MET     | the two records are built from different things — the residual from holdings, issuance and claims, the account from named events as they settle — so `AccountsBalance` compares two numbers and reports a signed, owned, sized difference. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/reporting.rs `Publishes` reads the account rather than the residual, so a published result is not the thing it is checked against                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Audit B5.b` | MET     | the residual is computed at read, and packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Equity` stores movements rather than their sum: `balance_of` adds up what one party's account was moved by, `moves` is the one write and there is none that sets it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Audit B6`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `NamesResolve` asks of every holding that its holder is a party in this world, that its line was issued, and that the line`s issuer is a party in this world. Existing and being alive are different questions and only the first is B6`s: a dead party`s estate holds and is held from, which is XI-8 working                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Audit B7`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `FlowsAreComplete` compares every non-money holding`s change against the legs that said why it moved, over instructions that SETTLED. It does not cover money: a money leg does not state where its units land, and a family re-deriving settlement`s routing would be reading its answer (Audit C3) — that half is `MoneyIsConserved`, per currency. Per-account money flows are unchecked, and stay so until settlement`s routing is a read a family can take                                                                                                              |
| `Audit B8`   | PARTIAL | all ten families are built and separately reported; the distinct-family logic test prevents two report identities collapsing into one. The long-run one-defect experiment remains measurement work.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Audit C1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Audit::run` (every family, every period, off ONE walk of the register — a family's independence is about the SOURCE it reads, not how many times the register is visited), packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `World::step` (**22e: it is IN THE PERIOD LOOP.** `audit.rs` and its families were built and tested and `grep audit assembly.rs` returned one hit, in a comment — the assembled world had stepped in every run since the port with no family ever visiting it. It runs last, over what the period left behind, and never repairs)                             |
| `Audit C2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `World::step` (the audit runs every period, last, over what the period left behind), packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Audit::run`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Audit C2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Audit::run` visits every family every period rather than sampling, so the first period a violation appears in is the period it is reported in                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Audit C3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Audit::run` runs the same assembled set of ten built families at every period close.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Audit C4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs (`run` takes `&Register` and every contribution takes `&Visit`, so a family cannot write; the test asserts the register is unchanged after a violation)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Audit D1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Report` (one per family, with its contributors and its violations)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Audit D2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Violation` (owner and size on every one), packages/kernel-rs/src/bin/world_runs.rs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Audit D3`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 1.5: no seeded generator exists in packages/kernel-rs. `Draw` in the bins is a counter-based sequence with a hard-coded start and nothing takes a seed value                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Audit D4`   | MISSING | a Part XII measurement, and it needs families that are built (VERIFICATION 1.4)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Audit E1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs (an absence has nothing to be inconsistent with — the audit takes only the register and the wire and can report nothing about a mechanism that does not exist)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Audit E2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `Audit::over` and `Family::ALL` keep absence explicit: any family removed from assembly is automatically reported NOT BUILT rather than silently green.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Audit E3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `NotBuilt` alongside `docs/COVERAGE.md` itself: a family that is green and a clause that is unmet coexist by construction                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
