| `Seed A4`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Seed A5`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 1.5: nothing in packages/kernel-rs takes a seed value. The one generator is `Draw`, redeclared in each of eight binaries with its own hard-coded constant                                                                |
| `Seed B1`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1: the populations in world_runs.rs are drawn from a counter and declared arbitrary                                                                                                                                    |
| `Seed B1.a` | MISSING | nothing seeds a population. packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs represents a cell with an integer weight, so an ensemble standing for a population is representable, and no weight anywhere is drawn to make a sum come out             |
| `Seed B2`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs gives a party an identity that survives the run. What is missing is the seed that would create them (VERIFICATION 2.1)                                                                              |
| `Seed B3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs (21e: each party is placed in a region, and `currency_of(region)` reads THROUGH the country that holds the money — so a region determines its money uniquely and keeps no copy of the fact, Law 4) |
| `Seed B4`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1: sizes in world_runs.rs come from `Draw::spread`, which is the arbitrary world, not a seed                                                                                                                           |
| `Seed B5`   | MISSING | there is no seed: `opening.rs` validates and builds a described opening state and nothing generates one, so no ratio is copied in because no ratio is drawn at all. The FORBID holds by the system being absent                       |
| `Seed C1`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1, 1.4: no opening state, and no Accounts family to check that it balances                                                                                                                                             |
| `Seed C2`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Seed C3`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Seed C3.a` | MISSING | no seed issues debt, so there is no opening maturity profile to spread or to concentrate. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs spreads what it issues itself across tenors, and it starts from a stock that has none         |
| `Seed C4`   | MISSING | nothing is seeded, so no opening price exists. packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `Provenance::Seeded` is the slot such a level would carry and no production code writes one                                                           |
| `Seed C4.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `Provenance::Seeded` marks a level nobody cleared and nothing writes one, so there is no seeded price that could fail to re-clear next period                                                        |
| `Seed C4.b` | MISSING | nothing separates an opening condition from a term, because nothing is seeded at all. packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs fixes a coupon once at `issue` and no table strikes one across the world                                  |
| `Seed C5`   | MISSING | no sector balance sheet is read at period zero, because there is no period zero to read one at: the arbitrary scale world is built by a counter and declared arbitrary                                                                |
| `Seed D1`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Seed D1.a` | MISSING | there is no period zero for period one to follow, so the first week is neither quiet nor a shock. Every number it produces comes out of the arbitrary opening state rather than out of a seed the flows agree with                    |
| `Seed D2`   | MISSING | `stores.rs` `Schedules` carries a covered interval per due, so an accrual position is representable; nothing states one at construction, and no opening instrument starts part-accrued                                                |
| `Seed D3`   | MISSING | a quiet first period is a measurement, and it needs a seed the flows agree with. The arbitrary world's first week is loud by construction: it reports 16,750 ownership violations and 1,357 flow violations from the moment it opens  |
| `Seed D4`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1: there is no seed, and the test the row cited is gone with the TypeScript engine                                                                                                                                     |
| `Seed E1`   | MISSING | nothing is seeded, so no outcome is. `prices.rs` `Provenance::Seeded` is the slot an opening level would carry and the arbitrary world writes none. The FORBID holds by the system being absent                                       |
| `Seed E2`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Seed E3`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1: there is no seed parameter to change, and the read the row cited is gone with the TypeScript engine                                                                                                                 |

## Currency

| requirement     | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Currency A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs (a currency is a named unit of account with an issuer), packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Currency A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs (21e: `Registry::currency(issuer)` is the only way to make one and there is no constructor without an issuer, so a money nobody owes cannot be written — Money A2 one level up from 5 A4's free money)                                                                                                                               |
| `Currency A3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/num.rs `MoneyAmount` carries a `CurrencyCode`, refuses `checked_add` across currencies and converts only against a named rate. It is not what the engine moves: packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs settles `Units`, a bare magnitude whose currency is read off the instrument beside it                                                           |
| `Currency A3.a` | PARTIAL | `MoneyAmount::checked_add` takes the currency along with the amount and refuses a mismatch, which is the shape the clause asks for. Every other arithmetic in the engine takes an `f64` and reads the currency from an instrument the caller passed separately, so the domain is checked by convention rather than by the type                                          |
| `Currency A4`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 5.2, 8.1: a leg carries a `ccy` that nothing checks against the instrument it names, and settlement infers where a payment lands from the payee`s bank without reading a currency at all                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Currency A5`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 2.1: there is no seed, so there is no named set of currencies. packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs can hold one; nothing states it                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Currency B1`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs `currency_of(region)` reads through the country (one fact, one writer). A party`s reporting in it is not built, and the `currency` module that would is imported by nothing                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Currency B2`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs holds a row per (party, money), so a foreign holding is a real position. VERIFICATION 8.1: it is not acquired by a trade — settlement converts at par on arrival                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Currency B2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `row` keys a holding by the money instrument and packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `ccy_of` gives that instrument exactly one currency, so a party has one account per currency it holds and two cannot share a balance                                                                                                          |
| `Currency B3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `across` compares the money`s currency with the payee`s account and REFUSES where they differ (`NoAccountInThatMoney`), so a payment in one money lands as that money or it does not land, and the decision to convert is a separate trade. What the payer short of that money does instead is not built: nothing buys the money first |
| `Currency B4`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 9.1: `SpotFx` proposes no instruction, so nothing is ever acquired from anybody at a price. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/currency.rs `short_of`/`MustBuy` is the shape and is dead code (10.1)                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Currency B5`   | MISSING | no bank runs a foreign position; VERIFICATION 9.1                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Currency C1`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 9.1: `SpotFx` computes a rate and journals it. No FX trade is ever proposed, so no rate is cleared from anything that settled                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Currency C2`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/currency.rs `Rates::of` inverts one print rather than storing two (Law 4). The store is dead code (VERIFICATION 10.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Currency C3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/currency.rs `gap` measures the triangle and `arbitrage` bounds who closes it — the right shape, and C3.b`s no-triangulating-read is honoured by `Rates::of` answering None. All of it is dead code (VERIFICATION 10.1)                                                                                                                |
| `Currency C3.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/currency.rs `Rates` is the single object the clause asks for and no production code enters that module at all, so no rate is read through it and no pair is read from anywhere else either                                                                                                                                            |
| `Currency C3.b` | MISSING | no pair clears, because packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs proposes no trade. Nothing triangulates through a chosen currency and nothing makes one cheapest, so the outcome the clause protects is not being produced at all                                                                                                                                  |
| `Currency C4`   | MISSING | there is no numéraire read anywhere in packages/kernel-rs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Currency C4.a` | MISSING | VERIFICATION 8.1: value does not live in a numéraire, but only because there is no numéraire; the currency position is destroyed by settlement converting at par instead                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Currency C5`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 8.1: there is no rate in force. Settlement converts at one and nothing values a foreign position at all (6.3)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Currency D1`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 6.3: no rate is in force for a period and nothing values against one                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Currency D1.a` | MISSING | no rate is in force for a period, so nothing can be valued at one and paid at another. packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `write` refuses a second print for one (market, instrument) in one week, which is where a single rate would live once one is cleared                                                                                                            |
| `Currency D2`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 6.3: there is no revaluation anywhere in packages/kernel-rs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Currency D2.a` | MISSING | nothing revalues a foreign position, so no difference reaches anybody's equity. No revaluation account exists on a currency issuer either, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/firms.rs computes equity as a residual with no place to put one                                                                                                                        |
| `Currency D2.b` | MISSING | VERIFICATION 6.3, 8.1: every foreign position is unrevalued, which is what this FORBID calls money created or destroyed silently                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Currency D3`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 6.3                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Currency D4`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 6.3, 1.4: nothing revalues and no family checks it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Currency E1`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 9.1: no rate moves because somebody traded at it; nobody trades                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Currency E2`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs clears a rate from posted orders rather than computing one, so no parity or differential formula produces a rate. What is absent is the market itself: `currency.rs` is wired into no system, and a rate nothing trades cannot be checked against a formula either                                                         |
| `Currency E2.a` | MISSING | no rate differential exists to be a reason for holding one currency over another, because no currency carries a rate. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/fx_forwards.rs computes a parity point and a basis and proposes no trade, so hedging costs nobody anything                                                                                                      |
| `Currency E3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/currency.rs has no written path and `Rates::of` refuses to invent one, which is the FORBID holding by absence. It holds in dead code (VERIFICATION 10.1)                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Currency E4`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 9.1: there is no flow to move a rate                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

## Bond

| requirement  | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `Bond N1`    | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs (`issue` asserts a named issuer; `issuer_of` reads it)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Bond N2`    | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs carries a `unit`, and the register counts units of it. VERIFICATION 7.1: there is no issued PRINCIPAL — no column records the amount owed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Bond N3`    | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `ccy_of` (one currency per line), and since 0t.5 a schedule row carries the money its amount is in, which `mechanisms/lending.rs` `Servicing` compares with the payer's account rather than inferring from it. What is still not true is that EVERY figure about a line is in that money — there is no currency-carrying amount type (Money A2.b), and the payer that owes a money it does not bank in has no way to buy it                                                        |
| `Bond N4`    | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `matures_on` (a Day on the one calendar)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Bond N5`    | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `coupon_of` is a fixed rate or `None` (N5.a and N5.c). N5.b has no representation: there is no margin, no reference-rate field, and nothing fixes a floating coupon — the `benchmarks` module that would print the fixing is imported by nothing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Bond N5.a`  | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs takes the coupon once at `issue` and `coupon_of` is the only read of it. No door writes one afterwards, so a fixed rate is locked at issuance because nothing could unlock it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Bond N5.b`  | MISSING | there is no floating coupon. `instruments.rs` has one `coupon: Option<f64>` and no reference rate; packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/equity.rs `Floating` brings a line and journals, and never fixes a coupon on one                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Bond N5.c`  | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `coupon_of` answers `None` for a discount line and `PaymentFrequency::AtMaturity` places no instalment, so a zero-coupon issue is representable and pays nothing until it repays. The discount never accretes: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `bill_accretion` is named by no production code                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Bond N6`    | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `PaymentFrequency` states how often an issue pays and `schedule_of` builds its dated rows from the issue's own terms, each amount the year fraction `Convention` gives for the interval it covers (G3.c) and each row carrying that interval, which is what makes accrual readable. The placement is short: `PaymentFrequency::weeks` answers a fixed count — four a month, thirteen a quarter — so a coupon lands where a count of weeks puts it and not where a date does (G3.a) |
| `Bond N7`    | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `Print` (per unit, in the instrument`s currency), packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs (written where a book cleared)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Bond N7.a`  | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `run_books` calls the solver once per market and instrument in a period and packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` crosses posted supply against posted demand. packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `write` then refuses a second print for that book in that week                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Bond N7.b`  | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs (there is no door that writes a price from a yield, a spread or an OAS; the FORBID holds by absence)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Bond N8`    | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `of_instrument` (who holds how many units). Re-marked from MISSING: the citation was absent, the mechanism is not                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Bond N8.a`  | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `HoldersAgainstIssued` sums units held against units issued, every period, and names the line and the size when they differ. A unit with no holder is a shortfall and one with two is a surplus, which is what B2.a and this clause both say                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Bond N9`    | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `Leg::Asset` with `Delivery::AgainstPayment` gives N9.a — the paper one way and the cash the other, in one atomic instruction. N9.b: packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `run_book` settles clean plus accrued, as a THIRD leg carrying `Receipt::Interest`, because what the seller earned on the coupon running now is interest and not the price. The print stays the clean price and the lot's basis stays the clean price; what the buyer pre-pays it is paid back on the date       |
| `Bond N9.a`  | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs pairs `Leg::Asset` with its money leg under `Delivery::AgainstPayment`, and `Settlement::attempt` pre-checks both before applying either, so the paper and the cash move in one pass or neither moves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Bond N9.b`  | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `accrued` computes what has run since the last payment and no trade adds it to what settles. The cleared price is the whole of what changes hands, so a coupon is a windfall to whoever holds the line on the date                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Bond N10`   | MISSING | nothing redeems an instrument. `instruments.rs` and `register.rs` have no door that ceases a line or empties its holdings                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Bond N11`   | MISSING | no instrument carries an early-termination regime, and there is no field in which "none" could be stated                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Bond N11.a` | MISSING | no instrument carries an early-termination regime, so there is nothing for an issuer to pay to hold one. packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs has no field in which a call, a make-whole or the price of either could be stated                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Bond N12`   | MISSING | no instrument carries a definition of default. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `Missed` is the shape and is dead code (VERIFICATION 11.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Bond N13`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/estate.rs `Rank` is the ordering, but no instrument states what its holder is entitled to on failure, and no claim is ever filed (VERIFICATION 4.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Bond N13.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/estate.rs `Rank` orders claims in a wind-up and no instrument states where its own holder stands. The ranking is a property of the waterfall rather than of the paper, so two issues cannot say they differ                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Bond N14`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `display` builds issuer + coupon + maturity and the id is never the name. It is called by packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/observer.rs `display_name`, which is itself dead code (VERIFICATION 11.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

## Derivative

| requirement        | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------ | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Derivative D1`    | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/derivative_layer.rs `agreed::DERIVATIVE` is a two-party agreement, so the two sides are named. Nothing makes it an asset to one and a liability to the other, because nothing marks a contract after inception, so neither side`s equity account is ever moved by what it is worth |
| `Derivative D1.a`  | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Agreements` (a relation names two parties and there is no constructor with one)                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Derivative D1.b`  | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `BilateralDerivativesAreZeroSum` checks that each contract resolves to two different live owners, which is the pairing and not the sum. No contract carries a value, so there is no pair of numbers to add to zero                                                                   |
| `Derivative D2`    | PARTIAL | a notional can sit in an agreement`s terms. `derivative_layer.rs gross_notional` is dead code (VERIFICATION 11.1), so D2.a`s distinction between notional and exposure is drawn nowhere                                                                                                                              |
| `Derivative D2.a`  | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/derivative_layer.rs `gross_notional` sums notionals and no production code names it, and nothing computes an exposure at all. With one of the two figures absent they cannot be told apart, and nothing reports either                                                             |
| `Derivative D3`    | MISSING | `AgreementTerms::PriceForward` types its underlying as an instrument, but no production mechanism strikes that contract, so no live derivative can yet be checked against a price this world clears                                                                                                                  |
| `Derivative D3.a`  | MISSING | there is no underlying field, so the FORBID has nothing to refuse                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Derivative D4`    | MISSING | no payoff function is carried on a contract; `derivative_layer.rs` variants are dead code                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Derivative D5`    | MISSING | derivative terms now carry a typed settlement currency, and FX forwards carry both currencies, but a general derivative still has no typed payment-leg schedule with a currency on each leg                                                                                                                          |
| `Derivative D6`    | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Agreements` carries an `until`, so a term can end. Payment dates on a leg are not represented, and `systems.rs` wires `derivative_layer::Derivatives`, but its production path does not perform this transition                                                                    |
| `Derivative D6.a`  | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Agreements` carries an `until`, so a term ends on a stated date. No derivative leg carries a periodicity or a day count, so a periodic payment on one would have no convention to accrue against                                                                                   |
| `Derivative D7`    | MISSING | nothing strikes a derivative at a cleared level; `Derivatives` proposes nothing and opens no book (VERIFICATION 9.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Derivative D7.a`  | MISSING | nothing strikes a derivative, so no level is cleared from two willing sides and none is solved for either. `Derivatives` opens no book and proposes no instruction                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Derivative D7.b`  | MISSING | no contract is struck at all, so no par strike is found by a mechanism. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/derivative_layer.rs holds no door through which a fixed rate or spread could be cleared                                                                                                                    |
| `Derivative D8`    | MISSING | VERIFICATION 6.3: nothing marks a contract after inception                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Derivative D8.a`  | MISSING | nothing marks a contract after inception, so no gain lands on one party and no loss on the other. The zero-sum family checks that a contract has two live sides and cannot check a sum nobody computes                                                                                                               |
| `Derivative D9`    | MISSING | `derivatives.collateral` is one of the three nouns with NO KERNEL HOME that `npm run world:runs` prints every run. `derivative_layer.rs initial_margin` and `variation` are dead code (VERIFICATION 11.1)                                                                                                            |
| `Derivative D9.a`  | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `pledge` is exactly the mechanism the clause names, taking units out of `free` while leaving them owned by the poster. No derivative posts anything, so the door is never used for one                                                                                            |
| `Derivative D10`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 4.1: no claim is ever filed against an estate, so an in-the-money party has nothing                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Derivative D10.a` | MISSING | no reservation anywhere carries a term for the counterparty it would face, and no derivative is quoted at all. A weak dealer therefore loses no flow, and who a party faces changes nothing about what it will pay                                                                                                   |
| `Derivative D11`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Agreements::ends` removes the relation from both parties at once. `derivative_layer.rs close_out` is dead code                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Derivative D11.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Agreements::ends` removes the relation from both parties at once, so a termination is one event rather than two. No close-out value is stated on the terms or computed at the end, so what the terminated contract was worth is not recorded anywhere                              |
| `Derivative D12`   | PARTIAL | an agreement is its own row and each derivative class has typed identity fields, so an underlying/reference can no longer be confused with a strike or currency. The store does not yet enforce economic-key uniqueness                                                                                              |
| `Derivative X1`    | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Agreements` is not the register, so a contract has no issuer and no issued amount and does not enter the ownership walk                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Derivative X2`    | MISSING | VERIFICATION 9.1: `Derivatives` proposes no instruction, so no margin, premium or periodic payment leaves any account                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Derivative X3`    | MET     | nothing prices an underlying off a contract, because nothing prices a contract at all (Derivative D8)                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
