| `Corporate Credit H4.a`  | MISSING | the same basis, read the other way: neither leg sets the other. It is refused by construction — a derivative on an uncleared price is forbidden and both books clear on their own — and what is not built is the MEASUREMENT of the gap. Positioned at 21.137 with H4                                                                                                                                   |

## Sovereign

| requirement      | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Sovereign A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` creates a tax due for the named statutory payer off each settled wage and sale leg, and typed public-purchase, transfer and employment agreements create the outlays. Both sides are named parties carrying dated dues rather than a rate on an aggregate                                                                                                             |
| `Sovereign A1.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` creates a tax due against the named party that remits it, off each settled wage and sale leg and off reported firm income, so no rate is applied to an aggregate. Only three bases exist: nothing taxes payroll separately                                                                                                                                            |
| `Sovereign A1.b` | PARTIAL | `Funding` creates purchases, transfers and public wages as dues to named payees, so three of the four kinds reach somebody. Interest is not among them: no sovereign coupon is an outlay the programme plans for                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Sovereign A1.c` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `Programme::to_raise` subtracts money actually held from what fell due and what was committed, and the money held is whatever remitted tax settled into the account. The gap is a leftover of two real flows, and nothing states a deficit to hit or reads one as an input                                                                                                     |
| `Sovereign A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `Programme::to_raise` sizes the need as redemptions plus outlays less the buffer, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `must_raise` reads that off the treasury`s own dated schedules before the books open, so the issue is brought ahead of the money leaving                                                                                                   |
| `Sovereign A2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `Programme` counts redemptions falling due beside what the treasury has committed to pay, so the need it raises against is both together and never the deficit alone                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Sovereign A2.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` sizes the week's issue from schedules that already exist and posts it before the outlays settle, so the plan is made against what is coming rather than against an empty account                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Sovereign A2.c` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` reads a tenor from `params` and issues at it, so a maturity is chosen. It is one declared tenor rather than a mix, and nothing weighs a cheap short issue that rolls against a dear long one that does not                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Sovereign A3`   | MET     | the treasury holds one money account like any other party and packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs pays every outlay out of it, recording `ShortOfMoney` rather than overdrawing it. There is no facility that clears a negative balance                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Sovereign A3.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs refuses a payment the balance cannot cover with `ShortOfMoney` and records the refusal, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `handle` takes the issuer to its buffer, a deferred outlay or back to the market. An empty account is an event with somewhere to go                                                                                                               |
| `Sovereign A3.b` | MET     | nothing advances money to a treasury. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/bank_funding.rs opens a central-bank facility to a bank and to nobody else, and a short treasury resolves through packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `handle` as buffer, then deferred outlay, then a return to the market. The absence is unguarded: no check refuses an advance, because no mechanism could make one                    |
| `Sovereign A4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `default_cause` makes non-payment in the sovereign`s own money a REFUSAL rather than an inability, and willingness is durable party-owned mandate state a decision moves. Default is therefore a choice and the choice is recorded                                                                                                                                             |
| `Sovereign A4.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs writes willingness through `now_stands` as durable state owned by that sovereign, and a decision moves it. It is a variable one party holds rather than a constant the model asserts                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Sovereign A4.b` | PARTIAL | `default_cause` makes non-payment in the issuer's own money a refusal and non-payment in a money it cannot create an inability, which is the distinction the clause calls the whole of sovereign credit risk. Nothing exercises it: a treasury owes only its own region's money                                                                                                                                               |
| `Sovereign B1`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `SovereignPaper::coupon` answers `None` for a bill and a rate for a bond, and each produces its own schedule — a zero-coupon line falls due in principal alone. They are still one `Class::Claim` distinguished by a field, which is the flag B1 says they must not be; and `bill_accretion`, which would accrete a bill against its own cleared price, has no caller          |
| `Sovereign B2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `default_cause` takes the issuer`s home currency and the currency the due is owed in, and that single comparison is what decides refusal from inability — the difference A4.b calls the whole of sovereign credit risk                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Sovereign B3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs keys a holding by (holder, instrument), so every holder of one line is fungible in it and the issued amount is one number. A re-opening that adds to an existing line rather than creating a new one is not built: each week`s funding brings a fresh line                                                                                                                                 |
| `Sovereign B3.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issue` allocates a new id every time and has no door that adds to an existing line, so every tap would be a separate instrument. Nothing re-opens anything, and the line and the issue are therefore the same object                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Sovereign B4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs carries no seniority on sovereign paper, so the ranking cannot vary, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Servicing` pays every holder of a line per unit of par out of one schedule. Pari passu is the construction rather than a rule laid over it                                                                                                              |
| `Sovereign B5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs reads a sovereign default only off a schedule whose state is `Failed` — a missed payment and nothing else. No covenant is struck on sovereign paper and no acceleration exists to trigger                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Sovereign B6`   | PARTIAL | no sovereign line carries a call, a make-whole or a non-call period, so there is no early-termination regime to exercise. The buyback and switch the issuer manages its curve with instead is `CurveOperation`, which has no caller                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Sovereign B7`   | MET     | a sovereign default opens a `SOVEREIGN_EXCHANGE` process in packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs and never an estate, so no holder acquires a claim on anything seizable. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/mortality.rs consumes a sovereign`s final payment failure without realising assets                                                                                                                      |
| `Sovereign C1`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `AuctionAnnouncement` carries the line and the size an auction is announced in, and nothing constructs one. The treasury brings its paper and posts in the same week, so no bidder ever sees a calendar ahead of the book                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Sovereign C1.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `AuctionAnnouncement` carries the line and the size an auction would be announced in, and nothing constructs one. No bidder sees a calendar, because none is published                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Sovereign C1.b` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `TreasuryIssues` posts the shortfall `must_raise` computed, so the size is the issuer's own and comes out of its programme. It is not announced ahead of the book, so nobody can prepare against it                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Sovereign C2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` returns one price every fill settles at, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs declares its auction `BuyersCompete`, so every winning bidder pays the stop-out. `sovereign.rs clear_uniform_auction` is a second solver for the same thing and is called by nothing — one of the two is a duplicate                                                                    |
| `Sovereign C3`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `DealerBid` carries a bidder, a level and a size, and no participant constructs one. No party holds the privilege, none is obliged to bid, and nothing wears the cost of bidding badly — so an auction cannot fail because the dealers stepped back                                                                                                                            |
| `Sovereign C3.a` | MISSING | no participant is a primary dealer, so nothing makes an auction hard to fail and nothing absorbs a remainder either. packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs already answers `NoDemand` where nobody bid, so the failure the obligation would make rare is representable and simply common                                                                                                                                         |
| `Sovereign C3.b` | MISSING | no dealer bids under an obligation, so none bids badly and none wears what that cost. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/dealing.rs `Dealers` posts where it chooses and is registered for no sovereign auction                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Sovereign C4`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs keeps every auction`s requested units, filled units and proceeds as a durable session, so the tail and the cover ratio are readable from it. Nothing reads them: neither statistic is computed or published                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Sovereign C5`   | PARTIAL | weak demand resolves as a worse price, because the treasury posts a size and the book clears where the bids reach. The other resolution is not built: `sovereign.rs handle` can return `ComeBackToTheMarket` at a different size or maturity and nothing acts on it                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Sovereign C6`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `run_book` settles an auction fill as an ordinary delivery-versus-payment instruction, so the proceeds land in the account of the treasury in the same pass the paper leaves it. A fill that cannot pay is a recorded failure and not a credited balance                                                                                                                                    |
| `Sovereign C7`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs reads `auction_of` for every session of the week and journals the issuer, the line, the ask, the fill and the proceeds wherever the fill fell short of the ask. Unsold paper stays with the issuer and the account is lower than the programme assumed                                                                                                                         |
| `Sovereign D1`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/systems.rs declares one book per sovereign line and packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs prints a price per unit where it crosses. No participant posts into a sovereign line after issue, so the secondary market has a venue and no parties                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Sovereign D2`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `annual_yield_from_price` derives the yield from the cleared price and the days to maturity, in that direction, and has no caller. No yield is published for any sovereign line, so nothing downstream can invert one either                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Sovereign D3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `curve_at` fits a tenor through observed points and is the one owner of the fit. It fits over points a caller hands it, and no caller builds those points from sovereign prints, so the curve is a function with no observations                                                                                                                                              |
| `Sovereign D3.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `curve_at` is the only fit in the tree and no other module derives a tenor point, so the curve has one owner and no second answer exists to disagree with it                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Sovereign D3.b` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `CurvePoint` carries a `CurveProvenance`, so a point says whether it was traded, interpolated, extrapolated or never traded, and `curve_at` never reads its own previous output. A consumer that needs a real price is not told: nothing reads the provenance before using a point                                                                                            |
| `Sovereign D3.c` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/calendar.rs `Convention` is the one place a compounding basis is stated and `year_fraction` the one function that applies it, so two conventions cannot answer one question. The curve does not publish which it used, so a consumer takes the convention rather than being told it                                                                                                                    |
| `Sovereign D4`   | MISSING | nothing prices as a spread to the sovereign curve. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs selects the lines whose issuer is a treasury and fits a curve; no lender, holder or issuer reads the result, so the benchmark benchmarks nothing                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Sovereign D5`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `pledgeable_value` is units times the cleared price less a haircut and has no caller. packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs can encumber units through a lien, and no repo pledges sovereign paper against them                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Sovereign D6`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `bid_offer` reads a width off the two best posted levels rather than applying a prior, and has no caller. No dealer quotes a sovereign line, so there is no width to be a consequence of anything                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Sovereign E1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `of_instrument` lists a line by its holders and `of_holder` lists a holder by its lines, so who holds how much of which sovereign line is one walk over one store. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs takes exactly that walk to read the holdouts on an open exchange                                                                                                         |
| `Sovereign E1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs is the one writer of a holding and indexes a line by its holders as well as a holder by its lines, so `of_instrument` answers the question in a single walk. Sovereign paper is a holding like any other and sits in no second store                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Sovereign E2`   | MISSING | the clause asks for six holder classes buying for six different reasons, which is what gives an auction two sides. None posts: no bank buys a liquidity buffer, no insurer buys duration, no central bank buys as policy, no foreign official buys reserves, no fund takes relative value and no household buys directly                                                                                                      |
| `Sovereign E2.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/bank_capital.rs weights an asset by its issuer's grade, which is the reason a bank would hold sovereign paper. No bank holds any, so the buffer motive reaches no book                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Sovereign E2.b` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/insurers.rs `InsurerMatching` bids from a price outlook rather than from a liability it must match, and no insurer holds a sovereign line. Duration is nobody's reason for anything                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Sovereign E2.c` | MISSING | no central bank holds sovereign paper, because packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `OpenMarketPurchase` is constructed by nothing. Policy is nobody's reason to hold a line                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Sovereign E2.d` | MISSING | no foreign official holder exists, because the world has one country and one currency. Nothing holds a line as a reserve                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Sovereign E2.e` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/funds.rs `FundMandates` carries what a fund may hold and no fund holds a sovereign line, so relative value is nobody's reason either                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Sovereign E2.f` | MISSING | no household or firm holds a bill directly, so no saver substitutes between a fund and the paper that fund would have bought. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/short_term_debt.rs `prefers_paper` is that choice and no production code names it                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Sovereign E3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `worth` is units times the latest cleared print for every holding, sovereign paper included, and returns missing rather than a basis where no market has printed. A holder`s sovereign position is marked the same way as anything else it owns                                                                                                                                         |
| `Sovereign E4`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `Leg::Pledge` encumbers named units and packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs refuses to move what is pledged, so paper CAN be pledged. `sovereign.rs pledgeable_value` applies the haircut and has no caller, and nothing pledges a sovereign line                                                                                                                                             |
| `Sovereign E5`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/bank_capital.rs weights an asset from the issuer`s grade through `classified_weight`, and a sovereign issuer is one `FailureMode::Never` cannot reach — so it weights lightest. It is not a declared zero, and no bank`s holding of sovereign paper is what puts it there: no bank holds any                                                                                                |
| `Sovereign F1`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Servicing` pays a due to every holder of record per unit of par, read off the register at the week`s open. The accrual half is absent: `sovereign.rs coupon_payments` has no caller, so a coupon is a step at its payment date and accrues to nobody in between                                                                                                                 |
| `Sovereign F2`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `bill_accretion` accretes a bill against that line`s OWN cleared price rather than off a curve, which is what the clause turns on, and it has no caller. No bill accretes                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Sovereign F3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `schedule_of` gives every dated line its principal payment from the contract, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Servicing` pays it out of the account of the treasury at maturity. The redeemed holding dies in that same instruction, so outstanding issuance falls through the one writer of the register                                                             |
| `Sovereign F4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `Programme` counts redemptions falling due in the need it must raise, so the issue that funds a maturity is brought into the same book as any other and takes whatever the market charges that week                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Sovereign F5`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `CurveOperation` carries the line bought in, the line switched into and what the operation costs, and nothing constructs one. The issuer never manages its own curve                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Sovereign G1`   | MISSING | the VERIFY asks that a sovereign short in its own money inflates rather than defaults. Neither half is reachable: there is no central bank to create the money and no price level a creation would move                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Sovereign G2`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `default_cause` separates the two: an issuer that is willing but owes money it cannot create fails by inability, and every other non-payment is refusal. The foreign branch is unreachable, because a treasury issues and owes only the money of its own region, so no sovereign is ever in the position the clause is about                                                   |
| `Sovereign G3`   | PARTIAL | a failed due opens a `SOVEREIGN_EXCHANGE` process on the LINE that failed rather than on the issuer, so a default is selective, and no estate opens because a treasury reaches none of the three doors in packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/mortality.rs that create one. The negotiation is absent: the process opens, restates its holdout face each week, and nothing offers terms or closes it                            |
| `Sovereign G4`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `holdout_face` sums the face standing outside the hands of the issuer for every open exchange, and the mechanism journals it each week. There is no offer to hold out from: no replacement line is proposed, no holder tenders, and no paper is exchanged                                                                                                                      |
| `Sovereign G5`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `TreasuryIssues` posts no market at all once a default event stands against the issuer, so the consequence is exclusion, and nothing liquidates it because no estate opens. The rating half is absent: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/ratings.rs grades a party off published accounts and no treasury publishes any, so a sovereign grade has neither producer nor consumer |
| `Sovereign H1`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `OpenMarketPurchase` carries the buyer, the seller, the line, the face and the price, and nothing constructs one. No central bank posts in any book, so no policy purchase is ever sized or made                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Sovereign H2`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `reserve_creation` values a purchase and refuses one whose seller is the issuer, and it is unreached. With no purchase there is no reserve created and the base does not move                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Sovereign H3`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `central_bank_remittance` is unreached. No central bank holds sovereign paper, so no coupon accrues on its book and nothing returns to the treasury                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Sovereign H3.a` | MISSING | no central bank holds a sovereign line, so nothing accrues on its book and no receivable stands on its sheet. packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `accrued` is the read that would produce the figure and no holder calls it                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Sovereign H4`   | PARTIAL | the forbidden side of the boundary holds, because a treasury raises before it spends and no advance reaches it. The permitted side does not exist: there are no open-market operations, so nothing is being drawn between and the policy choice the clause describes is not yet a choice anybody can make                                                                                                                     |
| `Sovereign H5`   | MISSING | the VERIFY compares two readings of one debt and there is nothing to read. No central bank holds a sovereign line, so neither the consolidated statement nor the accounting one has a subject                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Sovereign I1`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `BondFuture` names a deliverable line, a contract count, face per contract, a delivery week and the margin posted, and `future_settlement` marks it to the cash price of that bond. Nothing constructs one and no market in a future is declared                                                                                                                               |
| `Sovereign I1.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `net_basis` is the future price less the financed cash position, and it is unreached. There is no future price to measure against and nothing finances a sovereign line                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Sovereign I2`   | MISSING | the clause names three reasons to be on one side or the other and no participant posts a bond future at all. A duration mandate short of duration, a holder over its sovereign target and a dealer quoting both ways are absent from that book because the book does not exist                                                                                                                                                |
| `Sovereign I3`   | MISSING | nothing goes long a cash sovereign line against a short future, because neither leg exists: no future is constructed, and `Leg::Pledge` never encumbers sovereign paper to finance one. The repo demand the clause calls the largest real source of it is not in the model                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Sovereign I3.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `basis_trade_allowed` refuses a position whose posted margin does not cover the drawdown, and it is unreached. The FORBID holds only because no basis trader exists to lose anything                                                                                                                                                                                           |

## Short-Term Debt

| requirement            | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Short-Term Debt A1`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` brings paper with a tenor and a coupon read from `params`. A1.a`s discount instrument does not exist: every line it brings carries a coupon                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Short-Term Debt A1.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/short_term_debt.rs `Paper` carries a face, a price and two dates and no coupon at all, so the discount is the whole return by construction. Nothing accretes it over the life, so the return arrives entirely in the week the paper repays                                                                                     |
| `Short-Term Debt A1.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/short_term_debt.rs `Brings` reads its tenor in weeks from `params` and `Paper::term` is the distance between the week it was issued and the week it matures. The paper is short because its maturity is near, not because a flag says so                                                                                       |
| `Short-Term Debt A1.c` | MISSING | no instrument states where its holder ranks, so short-term paper cannot rank with an issuer's other senior debt. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/estate.rs `Rank` orders claims in a wind-up and no paper carries the rank it would be given                                                                                                                   |
| `Short-Term Debt A1.d` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issue` takes no early-termination term and no field holds one, so a bill has no option in it. The clause asks for none and the type provides none                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Short-Term Debt A2`   | PARTIAL | the price half holds: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/short_term_debt.rs `Brings` brings discount paper with no coupon into a declared book and packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs prints where it crosses. | `Short-Term Debt A2.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/short_term_debt.rs `Paper::yield_on` takes a `Convention` and hands it to `yield_to`, so a day count would be part of the answer, and no production code names it. Nothing derives a yield on short paper at all, so there is no number for a convention to be a material part of                                              |
| `Short-Term Debt A3`   | PARTIAL | the short borrower is the issuer and discount paper is priced by its book rather than a shared coupon; issuer-specific buyer limits remain incomplete                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Short-Term Debt B1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` reads what falls due inside its own window and brings paper for exactly that shortfall                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

## Equity

| requirement   | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `Equity A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/equity.rs (a share is the residual, ranking below all debt — packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/estate.rs `Rank::Equity` is last)                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Equity A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/estate.rs `Rank::Equity` is the last rank in the waterfall, so a shareholder is paid only once every debt claim has been                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Equity A1.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/equity.rs `residual` answers with two figures, what is left to shareholders and what is short to creditors, so a shortfall stays with the creditors and never becomes a claim on a holder                                                                                                                |
| `Equity A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `Class::Share` counted in its own unit, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/equity.rs `Line.outstanding` changed only by `apply`                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Equity A2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/equity.rs `Line::outstanding` moves only through `apply`, which takes a `ShareEvent` — issued, bought back, split or cancelled — so no path changes a count without naming what happened                                                                                                                 |
| `Equity A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `ccy_of` (a share is quoted in its issuer`s money)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Equity A4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs (a share is brought with `matures: None`, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/equity.rs says why there is no field to leave unset)                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Equity A5`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 12.1: the `equity` system runs `mechanisms/equity.rs Floating`, which brings a share line and journals; `mechanisms/equity.rs` is imported by nothing. No participant posts in an equity book, so no share price ever clears and nothing in §10 B–G happens                                                                   |
| `Equity A5.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/equity.rs `control_needs` is what a majority would cost in shares and no production code names it. | `Equity A5.b` | MISSING | VERIFICATION 12.1: the `equity` system runs `mechanisms/equity.rs Floating`, which brings a share line and journals; `mechanisms/equity.rs` is imported by nothing. No participant posts in an equity book, so no share price ever clears and nothing in §10 B–G happens                                                                   |

| `Equity B4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs and packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs (shares times price is computed where it is asked and stored nowhere)                                                                                                                                                                                              |

## Money Market

| ------------------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Money Market A1`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 12.1, 14.1: §11's mechanism says only what is outstanding in total. `MoneyMarketBanks` quotes `money_market.lends_at` and `money_market.borrows_at` — both declared 1.0 per annum — into an overnight book; `mechanisms/money_market.rs`, which holds the position, the corridor, the facility and the run, is imported by nothing |
| `Money Market A1.a` | MISSING | no bank lends reserves to another, so nothing is redistributed. packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `MoneyIsConserved` would see such a movement leave the total unchanged, and no interbank loan is ever proposed for it to see                                                                                                                    |
| `Money Market A1.b` | MISSING | nothing pairs one bank's deficit with another's surplus. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/money_market.rs `MoneyMarketBanks` posts a schedule and no book is declared for it, so the two sides never meet                                                                                                                                      |
| `Money Market A2`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 12.1, 14.1: §11's mechanism says only what is outstanding in total. `MoneyMarketBanks` quotes `money_market.lends_at` and `money_market.borrows_at` — both declared 1.0 per annum — into an overnight book; `mechanisms/money_market.rs`, which holds the position, the corridor, the facility and the run, is imported by nothing |
| `Money Market A2.a` | MISSING | no bank derives a buffer from how liquid its own funding is. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/bank_funding.rs reads a declared ratio from `params`, which is the floor the clause says may sit under a preference rather than the preference itself                                                                                            |
| `Money Market A2.b` | MISSING | VERIFICATION 12.1, 14.1: §11's mechanism says only what is outstanding in total. `MoneyMarketBanks` quotes `money_market.lends_at` and `money_market.borrows_at` — both declared 1.0 per annum — into an overnight book; `mechanisms/money_market.rs`, which holds the position, the corridor, the facility and the run, is imported by nothing |

## Spot FX

| requirement    | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| -------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `Spot FX A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `SpotFx` proposes both named currency legs in one atomic settlement instruction                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Spot FX A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs keys every production book and published clearing event by its explicit base and quote currencies                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Spot FX A3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/currency.rs measures direct-versus-cross inconsistency, but production clears each offered currency independently and no bounded arbitrage order consumes the gap                                                                                                                                              |
| `Spot FX B1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `SpotFx` nets foreign balances against due schedules and posts only the remaining currency shortfall                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Spot FX B2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `SpotFx` posts only the foreign-currency balance left after the holder`s own obligations                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Spot FX B3`   | MISSING | no portfolio allocator posts a currency order                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Spot FX B4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs reads each named party's due foreign-currency obligations and posts the residual exposure after its existing foreign balance                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Spot FX B5`   | MISSING | no dealer participant posts a two-sided FX schedule or owns currency inventory                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Spot FX B5.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `will_quote` is where a dealer would decline the flow it does not want, and no production code names it. Nothing quotes, so nothing is obliged either                                                                                                                                               |
| `Spot FX B6`   | MISSING | no central-bank policy decision posts a bounded FX order                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Spot FX C1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `Posted`/`clearing` take schedules in rate space; production reads the offered money instrument`s cleared print rather than casting a currency code to an instrument id                                                                                                                             |
| `Spot FX C2`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs clears each offered currency, but cross-pair consistency is neither a clearing constraint nor consumed by bounded arbitrage                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Spot FX C2.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs clears each offered currency on its own book, so consistency across pairs is neither a constraint the solver carries nor a correction applied afterwards. `inconsistency` measures the gap and no production code names it                                                                          |
| `Spot FX C3`   | MISSING | no dealer posts the two-sided schedules from which a bid-offer could emerge                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Spot FX C4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `clearing` walks the posted supply curve, so added demand reaches a different offered rate rather than a written path                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Spot FX C5`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs uses one clearing rate for both reciprocal settlement legs and journals it, but the rate is not yet a pair-keyed production price used by valuation                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Spot FX C6`   | MISSING | the production world has no pair-keyed history from which zero-flow drift and one-way-flow movement can be measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Spot FX D1`   | MISSING | the settled counterparty positions are real, but no FX dealer participant fills a client and owns the resulting inventory                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Spot FX D2`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `square` names a counterparty but no production dealer calls it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Spot FX D3`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs can calculate inventory revaluation, but no production dealer carries that inventory                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Spot FX D4`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs expresses a dealer inventory limit, but no production dealer consults it                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Spot FX D5`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs settles a spot exchange only as reciprocal `Receipt::Fx` legs in two currencies, so every trade has two sides and each currency conserves by construction. The VERIFY itself is not taken: `spot_fx.rs position_after` reads the identity and nothing calls it, so dealer and client positions are never summed |
| `Spot FX E1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs refuses self-matches; packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs recognizes an exchange only from two reciprocal `Receipt::Fx` legs in different currencies                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Spot FX E2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs (no parity formula, no differential applied to a level, no written path — the FORBID holds by absence)                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Spot FX E3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/currency.rs `arbitrage` bounds the arbitrageur by its own capital, which is the clause. It is dead code (VERIFICATION 10.1)                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Spot FX E4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs lands each reciprocal FX leg as its named money without routing it through the recipient`s home account                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Spot FX F1`   | MISSING | ordinary goods settlement still pays in the buyer`s account money rather than applying one seller-money convention                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Spot FX F1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs requires two reciprocal `Receipt::Fx` legs, so no conversion can occur without the named counterparty leg                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Spot FX F1.b` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs still chooses the buyer`s account instrument, so the settlement money changes with the buyer                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| `Fund Shares A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs and packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs (a fund is a named party with an account and rows)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Fund Shares A2`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/redeemable.rs `Subscribing` counts shares off `agreed::SUBSCRIPTION` terms. | `Fund Shares B2.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs marks a print stale rather than refreshing it, so a stale input is visible. ## CDS

| requirement | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CDS A1`    | MISSING | `Protection` now strikes a typed two-party CDS agreement naming its reference, spread, tenor and settlement currency. It still selects terms without a real CDS order book and does not schedule premium or protection cash flows, so the specified contract is not yet operating                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CDS A1.a`  | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/cds.rs `Protection` can name a reference entity, and no default event is defined for it. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `Missed` is the only thing in the tree that decides a payment was not made, and no contract reads it                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CDS A1.b`  | MISSING | no payoff is computed on an event, because no event triggers one. Par minus recovery needs a recovery, and nothing recovers anything: no estate has ever paid a claim                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CDS A1.c`  | MISSING | no running spread clears. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/cds.rs journals a premium from a declared number rather than from a book, and no book in protection is declared anywhere                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CDS A1.d`  | MISSING | no contract carries a tenor, so there is no curve of them and no term structure of credit anywhere in the tree. packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Agreements` carries an `until`, which is where a tenor would sit                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CDS A2`    | MISSING | VERIFICATION 12.1, 9.1: the `cds` system runs `mechanisms/cds.rs::Protection`, which is wired and can strike a typed CDS agreement from opposing views after an estate recovery exists; it does not schedule premiums, observe later reference events, or propose payouts, so `Contract`, `Series`, `Point`, `net_notional`, `owed_on_event`, `pays_out` and `unfunded_exposure` are unreachable. No CDS is ever struck and no spread ever clears |
| `CDS A3`    | MISSING | VERIFICATION 12.1, 9.1: the `cds` system runs `mechanisms/cds.rs::Protection`, which is wired and can strike a typed CDS agreement from opposing views after an estate recovery exists; it does not schedule premiums, observe later reference events, or propose payouts, so `Contract`, `Series`, `Point`, `net_notional`, `owed_on_event`, `pays_out` and `unfunded_exposure` are unreachable. No CDS is ever struck and no spread ever clears |
| `CDS A4`    | MISSING | VERIFICATION 12.1, 9.1: the `cds` system runs `mechanisms/cds.rs::Protection`, which is wired and can strike a typed CDS agreement from opposing views after an estate recovery exists; it does not schedule premiums, observe later reference events, or propose payouts, so `Contract`, `Series`, `Point`, `net_notional`, `owed_on_event`, `pays_out` and `unfunded_exposure` are unreachable. No CDS is ever struck and no spread ever clears |
| `CDS A4.a`  | MET     | a reference entity would have to be a party in this world; nothing writes protection on anything else, because nothing writes protection                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CDS A5`    | MISSING | VERIFICATION 12.1, 9.1: the `cds` system runs `mechanisms/cds.rs::Protection`, which is wired and can strike a typed CDS agreement from opposing views after an estate recovery exists; it does not schedule premiums, observe later reference events, or propose payouts, so `Contract`, `Series`, `Point`, `net_notional`, `owed_on_event`, `pays_out` and `unfunded_exposure` are unreachable. No CDS is ever struck and no spread ever clears |
| `CDS A5.a`  | MISSING | no index of reference names exists, so nothing is fixed at a roll and no weight settles out of a line on an event. packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs holds every index definition and none of them is a credit basket                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CDS A5.b`  | MISSING | no index book in protection is declared and no single-name book either, so there are no two prints for a basis to sit between. Nothing measures one and nothing sets one                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

## Commodities Spot

| requirement             | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Commodities Spot A1`   | PARTIAL | `mechanisms/commodities.rs Storing` reads each `Class::Good` line, its named production rows, settled consumption, physical stock and that line’s own cleared print, and packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs keys a print by (market, instrument, week), so the same grade in two places is two levels and `of_line` refuses to pick between them. The declaration is still one book per good rather than one per place, so no line has a second one yet |
| `Commodities Spot A1.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/commodities.rs `Grade` carries the place as part of what the good IS, so the same quality in two regions is two things. Both do not price: packages/kernel-rs/src/systems.rs declares one book per good rather than one per region and good, so there is one price and no transport difference between two                                                                                                          |
| `Commodities Spot A2`   | MET     | `goods.rs Making` creates output for the named firm that ran the recipe and destroys the named firm’s inputs; `commodities.rs Storing` reads those settled `Create` and `Destroy` legs by line and party                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Commodities Spot A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/commodities.rs `Storing` carries register inventory from one week into the next and proposes the storage payment to the live stockist at that location                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Commodities Spot A4`   | MET     | the register is the sole inventory store by named holder and instrument; `Storing` reads its live total and publishes physical tightness from settled consumption                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Commodities Spot B1`   | MET     | `goods.rs Making` chooses a firm-specific recipe from input, wage and capital-service costs; `commodities.rs Storing` reads the resulting named `Create` rows and derives observed supply at the cleared price from their costs                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Commodities Spot B1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Making` chooses a recipe from the input, wage and capital-service costs of that firm, so two producers facing different costs offer at different levels. The supply schedule is whatever those offers add up to and no curve is written anywhere                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Commodities Spot B2`   | MET     | `goods.rs decide` is constrained by the firm’s installed capital services and recipes complete only through `InProgress`; no commodity mechanism changes capacity inside the clearing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Commodities Spot B2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs holds output to the capital services a firm has installed and completes a recipe only through `InProgress`, so nothing can produce more inside the week the book clears. Price is what moves instead                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Commodities Spot B3`   | MISSING | `goods.rs` records normal recipe waste as physical units and `commodities.rs disrupted` expresses a physical capacity loss, but no production caller supplies a disruption event                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Commodities Spot B4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `GoodsSellers` offers only at the reservation price of the producer, and what does not sell stays in its register holding. Nothing empties an inventory at whatever it fetches                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Commodities Spot C1`   | MET     | `goods.rs Making` destroys the exact inputs a named firm’s selected recipe needs, and `HouseholdBuyers` purchases its declared consumption basket                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Commodities Spot C2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Making` fixes the input units a recipe wants and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/households.rs `HouseholdBuyers` fixes the basket, both before the book runs. Neither reads the price that book is about to clear at                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Commodities Spot C3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Stockist` submits funded orders from its own `price_outlook`, competes in the same physical books as everybody else and takes delivery of the same units                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Commodities Spot C4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` crosses stepped quantity-backed orders from producers, consumers and stockists. No volatility figure exists anywhere, so scarcity moves the price only by changing which posted level is the one that crosses                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Commodities Spot D1`   | PARTIAL | `session.rs` clears one book at a time and packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs keys what it prints by the market as well as the line, so two places in one grade keep two runs and a reader that names neither is refused rather than served the wrong one. What is short is the declaration: one book per good is opened, so the second place has no book to clear in                                                                                   |
| `Commodities Spot D2`   | MET     | inventory is the register’s carried state: `GoodsSellers` and `Stockist` can offer only free units they actually hold, so its presence or exhaustion changes the sell schedule seen by clearing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Commodities Spot D2.a` | MET     | inventory is the carried state of the register rather than a number beside it: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `GoodsSellers` and `Stockist` can offer only free units they actually hold, and production and consumption are what move that holding. What is left therefore changes the sell schedule the book sees                                                                                                                       |
| `Commodities Spot D3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/commodities.rs `Storing` charges every live holder per unit and proposes the payment to the named stockist that owns storage in the location of that holder                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Commodities Spot D4`   | MISSING | spot now clears from physical orders and `Storing` reads storage cost and the cleared print, but the futures production path is not wired yet, so no traded forward relationship exists                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Commodities Spot D5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/commodities.rs `CommodityUnits` contributes to the units audit, reconciling settled creation plus prior register stock against settled destruction plus current stock, one physical line at a time                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Commodities Spot E1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `costs` reads cleared input prices through the view of the firm when it chooses a recipe, and those input units are then destroyed with their carried cost entering the output lot                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Commodities Spot E2`   | MET     | `HouseholdBuyers` buys the registry-declared basket, including energy/commodity lines, with its own cash in the same physical books                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Commodities Spot E3`   | MISSING | `cross_border.rs` preserves party-to-party physical export flows, but commodity terms of trade are not consumed by the currency-fundamentals decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Commodities Spot E4`   | MISSING | the physical price feeds firm input cost and household consumption, but the complete margin-to-inflation-to-policy chain has no wired monetary-policy consumer yet                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Commodities Spot F1`   | MET     | `ledger.rs` pre-checks every `Destroy` against free units, so consumption without settled production or inventory is refused                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Commodities Spot F2`   | MET     | `ledger.rs` refuses a `Destroy` beyond the holding as `ShortOfUnits`; the register cannot become negative through the wire                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Commodities Spot F3`   | MET     | a commodity price is written only by packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `run_book` out of a completed session, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/commodities.rs only reads the cleared print. No price path is written anywhere                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
