## Banks Lending

| requirement          | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| -------------------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Banks Lending A1`   | PARTIAL | `agreed::COMMITMENT` is a named lender to a named borrower and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Rows` is a book of loan rows. Nothing originates one, and the origination IS the wiring                                                                                                             |
| `Banks Lending A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs keeps a loan as an `agreed::COMMITTED_CREDIT` between two named parties rather than as an instrument in the register, so it has no issue, no holders and no book. Nothing can price or transfer it                                                                              |
| `Banks Lending A1.b` | PARTIAL | a loan sits in `Agreements` and never in the register, so it is not marked with the instruments that are. ## Banks Capital

| requirement          | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| -------------------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Banks Capital A1`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `equity` is a residual and is never a stored pot, and `booked_equity` reads every position under the treatment its holder declared. A bank that declared `Carrying::Cost` is not moved by a mark, which is the mechanism XI-6 describes rather than a gap; the loss reaches it on the day it sells, and nothing forces that sale yet    |
| `Banks Capital A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `equity` is computed at every read and nothing withdraws from it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Banks Capital A2`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 9.1: `BankCapital` reads a bank position against two declared ratios and journals. It proposes nothing, and `mechanisms/bank_capital.rs Absorbed`, `Binding`, `Conservation`, `absorb`, `hole`, `trigger` and `no_creditor_worse_off` have no caller (11.1). Nothing recapitalises, nothing is resolved, nothing is bailed in                                    |
| `Banks Capital A2.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/estate.rs `Rank::Equity` is last in the waterfall, so equity is what a loss reaches first. It absorbs nothing in practice, because no loss is booked against a bank's equity outside a wind-up                                                                                                                                              |
| `Banks Capital A2.b` | MISSING | no instrument states a rank, so no subordinated layer exists between equity and senior paper. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/estate.rs `Rank` has the ordering and nothing issues into it                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Banks Capital A2.c` | MISSING | no resolution reaches senior creditors or depositors, because nothing values the book a resolution would take. The ladder has no rung for them to be last on                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Banks Capital A3`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 9.1: `BankCapital` reads a bank position against two declared ratios and journals. It proposes nothing, and `mechanisms/bank_capital.rs Absorbed`, `Binding`, `Conservation`, `absorb`, `hole`, `trigger` and `no_creditor_worse_off` have no caller (11.1). Nothing recapitalises, nothing is resolved, nothing is bailed in                                    |
| `Banks Capital A4`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 9.1: `BankCapital` reads a bank position against two declared ratios and journals. It proposes nothing, and `mechanisms/bank_capital.rs Absorbed`, `Binding`, `Conservation`, `absorb`, `hole`, `trigger` and `no_creditor_worse_off` have no caller (11.1). Nothing recapitalises, nothing is resolved, nothing is bailed in                                    |
| `Banks Capital B1`   | PARTIAL | `bank.min_weighted` = 0.08 and `bank.min_leverage` = 0.03 are declared POLICY primitives owned by the parliament, and `BankCapital` reads them. Nothing weights an asset, so B1.a`s risk weights do not exist — no value reaches a balance sheet to be weighted                                                                                                               |
| `Banks Capital B1.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/bank_capital.rs `classified_weight` reads the grade of an issuer, so two assets can weigh differently. No bank holds anything it weighs, so no preference between assets ever follows from it                                                                                                                                               |
| `Banks Capital B1.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/bank_capital.rs `BankCapital` reads `bank.min_leverage` beside the weighted ratio and tests the unweighted position against it, so the backstop is a second constraint and never a function of the first                                                                                                                                    |
| `Banks Capital B1.c` | MISSING | VERIFICATION 9.1: `BankCapital` reads a bank position against two declared ratios and journals. ## Treasury

| requirement     | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Treasury A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs `kinds::TREASURY` with an account it pays out of, and packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs refuses a payment it cannot make, so A1.a`s balance can run low                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Treasury A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs pays every treasury outlay out of the balance it holds and refuses one it cannot cover with `ShortOfMoney`, so the balance is real and running low is a recorded refusal rather than a negative number                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Treasury A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs `currency_of(region)` read through the country                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Treasury A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `of_holder` is the cash and the instruments a treasury owns and packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `of_issuer` is every line it issued, so the balance sheet is a read of two indexes. No equity figure is stored anywhere, which is what keeps A3.a a read                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Treasury A3.a` | MET     | no equity figure is stored for a treasury anywhere, so what it is worth is `of_holder` less what `of_issuer` says it owes, computed when somebody asks. Nothing refuses the answer for being negative                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Treasury B1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` turns each live public-purchase, public-transfer and treasury-employment agreement into a scheduled payment to its named producer, beneficiary or worker                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Treasury B2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs schedules each issued line’s coupon on that line, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Servicing` reads current holders and pays each holder pro rata                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Treasury B3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` reads separately decided purchase, transfer and employment agreements rather than a constant outlay; its named-payer tax bases move with settled wages, sales and reported profit                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Treasury B3.a` | MISSING | no outlay moves with the cycle, because nothing measures one and no transfer responds to a household losing work. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs reads a declared per-head transfer from `params`, so outlays are a constant and the constraint never tightens from that side                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Treasury B4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Servicing` distributes principal due on a maturing line across every current holder and retires each delivered holding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Treasury C1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` levies income and payroll tax on each settled wage, consumption tax on each settled sale, and profit tax on each named firm’s own lagged account statement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Treasury C1.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` creates a tax due against each named payer and ordinary settlement debits that payer's own account, so the flow is real at both ends. The base for the corporate tax is the income packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/firms.rs reported, which is the payer's own statement; the wage and sale bases are read off settled legs rather than off anything the payer stated                       |
| `Treasury C2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` derives a tax due from settled money legs and from reported firm income, so receipts move with the bases that produced them and fall when those bases fall                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Treasury C3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs creates one tax schedule for each named statutory payer, and `TreasuryIssues` reads receipts only by summing schedules owed to that treasury                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Treasury D1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` reads outgoing and incoming schedules plus cash and the durable buffer before bringing paper; `TreasuryIssues` repeats that live read when it sizes the auction order                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Treasury D2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `TreasuryIssues` posts a sell into a book that must clear, and packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs settles what crosses                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Treasury D2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `TreasuryIssues` posts a size and a maturity and no level of its own where the venue lets the buyers compete, so the issuer chooses what and how much and packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs decides at what price                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Treasury D3`   | MET     | nothing advances money to a treasury. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `must_raise` sizes the gap from what the account actually holds, packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs refuses an outlay the balance cannot cover with `ShortOfMoney`, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/bank_funding.rs `facility_borrower` admits only a bank — so a shortfall stays a shortfall and no facility creates a claim of a central bank on a treasury |
| `Treasury D3.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `central_bank_buys` refuses a purchase whose seller is the issuer, which is exactly the line between a policy purchase and an overdraft, and no production code names it. No central bank buys anything, so the permitted act never happens                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Treasury D4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` reads every payment falling in its forward window against incoming schedules, current cash and the treasury’s durable buffer mandate before it brings dated paper                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Treasury D4.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `rollover_exposure` reads what falls due inside a window and no production code names it, and every issue carries one declared tenor so the profile is a single wall by construction. Nothing is pre-funded                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Treasury D4.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `must_raise` subtracts the mandated buffer from what the treasury holds before sizing an issue, so the buffer is money kept back rather than a line in a plan, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `handle` draws on it first when an auction falls short                                                                                                                                |
| `Treasury D5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `run_book` settles only crossed quantity, so unsold paper stays with the issuer, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `handle` takes what is still short to the buffer, then to a deferred outlay, then back to the market, in that order                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Treasury D5.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` obliges nobody: it crosses the levels that were posted and leaves the rest unfilled. An order naming no level is admitted only as auction supply under `BuyersCompete`, so no bid can be the residual buyer of a remainder                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Treasury D6`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `issued_of` is what a line has outstanding and packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `held_total` is what is held of it, and issuance and redemption are the only writers of either. The ownership audit reads both and reconciles them                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Treasury E1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` chooses the declared tenor for each newly dated issue and the resulting maturity profile remains on the issued lines rather than in a debt-total field                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Treasury E2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` runs the programme every week and sizes that auction from `must_raise` against live cash and live schedules, taking its maturity from the declared tenor                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Treasury E3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `write` retains each auction print with its provenance and packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `schedule_of` retains the dated cash flows the issue promised. The cost of the debt is therefore derived from what was transacted, and no party sets it                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Treasury E4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `TreasuryIssues` puts the whole live shortfall into the quantity of its sell order, so a heavier size meets unchanged bid schedules in the same call book and moves the print it clears at                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Treasury F1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` creates purchases, transfers and wages as bilateral dues, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Servicing` settles them as money received by the named counterparty                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Treasury F2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs `Funding` creates tax as a bilateral obligation of each named payer, and packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs `money_delta` debits the money that payer itself holds                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Treasury F3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `schedule_of` puts the interest on the sovereign line, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Servicing` reads `of_instrument` on the payment date and delivers to every current holder                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Treasury F4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs `Instruction` carries every fiscal payment as one atomic money leg between a treasury and a private counterparty, and the flows audit reads those same settled legs. The fiscal balance and private net saving are two views of one transaction rather than an enforced identity                                                                                                                                     |

| `Goods A2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs `Way` states a fixed quantity of each input per unit of output and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs consumes exactly that, so a firm short of one input cannot make up the difference with another. | `Goods A2.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs draws `per x units`, never money divided by a price, so a price doubling does not halve the draw                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `Reporting A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/reporting.rs `Publishes` publishes a result per firm on a calendar placed by the day the company started, and A1.a IS read: it walks every living party, sums what outsiders hold of each share line off packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs, and asks `reporting::reports(listed, outsiders)`. There is no kind of party that reports (Law 15). The row said the opposite until 0j.7 read the source                                                                            |
| `Reporting A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/reporting.rs `Publishes::run` derives public status each period from a listed share line and outsider holdings in the register; no reporting party kind is consulted                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Reporting A2`   | PARTIAL | `Publishes` reads the equity account and the journal, so G2`s "the equity account`s movement over the fiscal period" is read off the record the events moved. What it reads is the account as it stands today rather than as it stood at the fiscal close, because a date-week has no inverse on the one calendar                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Reporting A2.a` | MET     | `Publishes` composes nothing: every figure is a read of the register and the journal                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

## Expectations

| requirement         | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `Expectations A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Outlooks` keys an outlook by party and by subject and `form` refuses one with no holder, while packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/expectations.rs `Forming` builds the things a party acts on: what it sells for, what it keeps earning, what credit costs, how much it sells and whether a name pays it back                                                                                                               |
| `Expectations A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/expectations.rs `Forming` forms each party`s outlook from the prints on the lines IT holds and what IT delivered, and from nothing else                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Expectations A2.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/observer.rs journals a count and publishes no aggregate, so there is no statistic for a party to observe. Nothing lags, nothing revises, and no outlook has a published number among its observations                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Expectations A2.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Outlooks` is keyed by party; there is no global expectation anywhere                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Expectations A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `price_outlook` and `outlook` answer for the asking party alone, so two parties with different histories read different numbers into the same book and the two sides of it are their disagreement                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Expectations A4`   | MET     | `Forming` corrects last period`s outlook toward what was observed. Nothing runs the world forward                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Expectations A5`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Outlooks` carries a level and no unit or horizon; `about::` names the subject and not the periodicity                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Expectations B1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs `with_seed_and_memory` draws one reproducible memory horizon per party at admission, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/expectations.rs `Forming` corrects each outlook towards what happened at the speed of that party                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Expectations B1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs `with_seed_and_memory` gives each party one memory drawn at admission from a declared range, so members of one sector weigh different numbers of their own periods. It is the only preference the system takes and nothing redraws it                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Expectations B1.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `observe` is the one writer of an outlook and it takes an observation, so nothing else moves one. `confidence` is derived from the recorded surprises rather than held, and no anchor, target or sentiment figure is stored anywhere                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Expectations B2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Outlooks::observe` records party, subject, period, expected and observed before adapting the level                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Expectations B2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `forecast_errors` returns the surprises as dated rows readable by party and by subject, so an outlook that moved can be put beside the surprise behind it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Expectations B3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `confidence` derives the width from each party`s own recent absolute forecast errors; dealing consumes it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Expectations B4`   | MET     | `Forming` reads the last outlook and this period`s prints, never the period`s own result of the decision it feeds                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Expectations B5`   | PARTIAL | the mechanism the VERIFY needs is there: packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs disperses the memory horizon across parties and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/expectations.rs `Forming` corrects at the speed of each one, so lags differ by party by construction. The measurement against a turning point has not been taken                                                                                                                                |
| `Expectations C1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/households.rs `HouseholdBuyers` bids from the `price_outlook` of the cell itself, its spendable cash and the buffer it keeps, so a cell expecting a fall bids lower out of the same money                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Expectations C2`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Making` reads the firm`s own `HOW_MUCH_IT_SELLS`for the production decision, packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/capital_programme.rs`Building` reads it for the investment one and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/employment.rs reads it for hiring — a firm with no view does not act on somebody else`s. It is one outlook doing all three: the price it expects to sell at is declared and nothing reads it |
| `Expectations C2.a` | MISSING | the clause asks for an outlook a party holds ABOUT another party, published and weighable. `repayment_of` and `loss_given_failure_of` key exactly that, and nothing forms either: no bank estimates a company's coming report and no such estimate is published                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Expectations C3`   | MET     | every participant builds its reservation from `price_outlook` and its own position, and packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `run_book` writes the print only after the book has cleared. The view that meets a book was therefore formed before that book produced anything                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Expectations C4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `repayment_of` keys a lender outlook to the named borrower and `Forming` moves it from what that lender saw of that name, while packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/dealing.rs `Dealers` quotes around the `price_outlook` it holds on the line it makes                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Expectations C5`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs reads its own `price_outlook` on the line it is about to auction, so the public sector gets no better forecast than anybody else. The central-bank half is absent: no such party exists to hold an outlook or to read one                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Expectations C6`   | MISSING | see C1, and Polity: no cell votes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Expectations D1`   | MET     | `Forming` reads only the party`s own rows and the public prints on them                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Expectations D2`   | MET     | `Forming` forms per party, cells included, and nothing averages across them                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Expectations D3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `observe` scores every outlook against what actually arrived and keeps the surprise, and `forecast_errors` hands those scores back. An outlook nothing scores cannot exist, because scoring is the only thing that moves one                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Expectations D4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/expectations.rs `About` admits an income, a price on a named line, a rate and a quantity, and nothing else. No outlook can be about a mood, an index or a survey, so no aggregate of outlooks is available to be a cause                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Expectations E1`   | MISSING | a Part XII measurement; there is no dispersion to read while every reservation is the same constant (VERIFICATION 13.2)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Expectations E2`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 13.3: outlooks are formed correctly by `mechanisms/expectations.rs Forming` and NOTHING READS THEM — `grep outlooks() systems.rs` is empty. `mechanisms::expectations` is imported by nothing (12.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Expectations E3`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 13.3: outlooks are formed correctly by `mechanisms/expectations.rs Forming` and NOTHING READS THEM — `grep outlooks() systems.rs` is empty. `mechanisms::expectations` is imported by nothing (12.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Expectations E4`   | MISSING | VERIFICATION 13.3: outlooks are formed correctly by `mechanisms/expectations.rs Forming` and NOTHING READS THEM — `grep outlooks() systems.rs` is empty. `mechanisms::expectations` is imported by nothing (12.1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `Geography A1` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs declares a finite grid of tiles, each with one identity and one coordinate. | `Geography A2` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `Surface` is land or water and has no third value, so an unknown surface is unconstructible rather than refused. No world declares a tile, so no surface exists to be either                                                                                                        |
| `Geography A3` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs holds one adjacency read over the grid. Nothing reads it, so nothing can reconstruct it differently either: freight keeps no routes and the observer publishes no map                                                                                                               |
| `Geography A4` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs carries kilometres and square kilometres as their own types and keeps `TileId` an identity that is never a distance. Nothing in this world measures a length, so the distinction is declared and unexercised                                                                        |
| `Geography A5` | MISSING | nothing keeps a second map for presentation because nothing keeps a first, and no terrain constrains a site or a route. The FORBID holds by the system being absent                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Geography B1` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs derives a physical length in kilometres from the declared projection and world scale, and no caller asks it for one                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Geography B2` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs sums a path's real legs rather than counting grid steps, and has no caller. Elsewhere in the world a region is an id with no extent, so nothing has a length to mistake                                                                                                             |
| `Geography B3` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs carries surface, slope and the infrastructure actually present on a tile. No carrier observes any of it: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/freight.rs `Carriage` counts live agreements and reads no terrain                                                                        |
| `Geography B4` | MISSING | symmetry, zero self-distance and leg adjacency are asserted at logic level in packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs`s own tests. The VERIFY is a standing read over a world and there is no world to take it over: no audit contribution exists                                                                           |
| `Geography B5` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs refuses a route leg whose ends are not neighbours and a land leg over water without a declared crossing. The refusal has no caller, so it is written and never exercised                                                                                                            |
| `Geography C1` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `declare_country` is unreached. The countries this world has are packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs rows over no tiles — and registry.rs declares its **own** `CountryId` beside geography.rs`s, which is two representations of one country with the read one holding no territory |
| `Geography C2` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `assign` is unreached, so no tile belongs to a region. The type does keep international water and an unassigned tile as different states, which is the distinction the clause turns on                                                                                              |
| `Geography C3` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `tiles_of` would enumerate a region and is unreached. A region in the running world is a packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs id with no extent, so the distance between two regions is not a read of sites and paths — it does not exist at all                                      |
| `Geography C4` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs places a party, plant, dwelling, warehouse or port on an exact tile and is unreached. packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs carries a region id on the party instead, so country and region are read FROM the party rather than THROUGH a site                                          |
| `Geography C5` | MISSING | buying a factory cannot move it, because no factory has a site to be moved from. | `Geography C6` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `jurisdiction_of` is the read this VERIFY would take and is unreached. Nothing generates a terrain, so no such record is ever written                                                                                                  |
| `Geography D2` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs generates from its declared inputs and no clock or ambient source, so reproducibility is a property of the function. It is never run, so two runtimes have never been compared                                                                                                      |
| `Geography D3` | MISSING | nothing partitions land into countries and regions, so no partition targets a price, an output, a wealth or a trade balance.
